## RVA 0x002d65e0

**Initialize the native float-setting object** · Level B · preferred VA `0x1402d65e0`

Writes the initial setting and its shadow value.

The inspected constructor writes the live float at object byte offset +0x08 and the initializer shadow at +0x10. The authored settings report distinguishes these operands from the loaded setting that the generation path consumes. Neither the constructor nor the loader proves an effective value in a historical session.

## RVA 0x00787830

**Construct and register the manager's ProcGen owner** · Level B · preferred VA `0x140787830`

Stores the manager's ProcGen pointer and registers it for module dispatch.

The mapped constructor writes the pointer at manager `+0x130` and appends that same object to the module vector at `+0x170/+0x178`. This grounds the runtime population owner and reset-dispatch relation. It does not identify a generated region's geographic source or extend its lifetime. The full constructor declaration and every registration prerequisite remain unknown.

## RVA 0x00789ce0

**Install a runtime manager owner and descriptor** · Level B · preferred VA `0x140789ce0`

Selects reset or reinitialization behavior from actual owner and component inputs.

The mapped owner-install path resets on zero owner, resets an existing nonzero context when owner changes, and tests current versus incoming descriptor component for equal owner. Initialization through `0x14078b030` rebuilds landscape. This context controls the runtime owner, separate from the geographic source fields and per-allocation lifetime. Exact calling contract and all callers remain unknown.

## RVA 0x0079a330

**Attempt a per-cell generated resource region** · Level B · preferred VA `0x14079a330`

Analysis labels recorded in the original sources are `FUN_14079a330`.

Performs a bounded resource-region attempt and can append into the holder's resource collection.

Traversal `0x14079c470` reaches this per-cell attempt. The mapped resource key is stored at `0x14079a615`, followed by holder resource lookup and possible insertion. Region append `0x1407ac2f0` returns a collection index or `0xffffffff`. The observed `R12B` marker can be set even after an append failure, so the marker does not establish retained nonempty geometry. The historical register scopes its CRC/MT interpretation to a conditional first gate. General native declaration and complete generation arithmetic remain unknown.

Source contract from [native availability map · functions](../experiments/native-availability-map/functions.csv) (line 22).

- **Inputs** — Resource; cell key; owner key; descriptor; weight; origin; clipping; prior regions
- **Outputs** — Accepted ordered points/widths and regions
- **Conditions** — CRC/MT state and competition population/order
- **Effects** — RNG and geometry/output mutation
- **Mapped edges** — 14079c470 -> this ->1407af1a0
- **Limits** — Only first gate executable in prior model; full generator equivalence untested
- **Source assessment** — STATIC instruction/dataflow; STRONG scoped interpretation proposed

## RVA 0x0079b520

**Generate regions into a source holder** · Level A · preferred VA `0x14079b520`

Analysis labels recorded in the original sources are `FUN_14079b520`.

Uses source-local and geographic query inputs to populate a resource holder.

Observed generator operands are `RCX` source, `RDX` source-origin `Int2` pointer, `R8` geographic query pointer, `R9D` owner value, then BIOM and output holder-context pointers at entry `RSP+0x28` and `+0x30`. It resolves the owner's component using the source BIOM key and checks body/raster readiness. Ordinary sample `0x142339340` returns the low nibble in `AL`. That nibble affects an ordinary numeric rarity comparison, with `8` selecting the inactive multiplier. Special generation has a distinct high-nibble path. Successful lookup, sampling and generation-return are weaker observations than retained valid region data. The public function declaration and general return meaning remain unknown.

Source contract from [native availability map · functions](../experiments/native-availability-map/functions.csv) (line 17).

- **Inputs** — Source descriptor pointer; transformed Int2 origin; coordinate representation; manager+80 key; BIOM; output wrapper
- **Outputs** — Resource region map if component/database/selection prerequisites permit
- **Conditions** — Current component store; per-BIOM match; BiomeMapDB state; selected descriptors; source bounds/frame; RNG/order
- **Effects** — Allocates/sorts descriptors and constructs geometry
- **Mapped edges** — 140771f30; 14079c540/5e0; 1407af470; 14079c470
- **Limits** — Void return; failed component or database-state branch exits; source pointer is not owner key; status0/6 not named enums
- **Source assessment** — STATIC instruction/dataflow; STRONG scoped interpretation proposed

## RVA 0x0079f860

**Provide source population for a terrain query** · Level A · preferred VA `0x14079f860`

Analysis labels recorded in the original sources are `FUN_14079f860`.

Selects source contributors and conditionally reuses or constructs a ProcGen population.

The observed entry takes ProcGen in `RCX`, the original signed `Int2` pointer in `RDX`, and a caller aggregate output-holder address in `R8`. Density builder `0x1407a1940` and layer worker `0x1407b5160` are qualified callers. The provider retains the Grid, selects a cell and enumerates raw source pointers. A token read from source `+0x68` is checked against the ProcGen visited collection under shared protection, then the unvisited path looks up the full source pointer in the map under exclusive protection. Visited hit and map hit are different outcomes. On map miss a holder is inserted before calling generator `0x14079b520`, and a later visited reserve can fail independently. A retained Grid, map insertion, visited token or provider return alone does not establish the source lifetime or complete nonempty geometry. `RAX` has no validated population-success interpretation.

Scoped pseudocode abstraction. It covers the described branch and omits unqualified paths.

```text
Abstraction of the ordinary source-population decision. Query preparation, output aggregation and cleanup are omitted.

retain Grid and enumerate the requested cell's sources
for each eligible source:
    if shared_visited_scan(source.token) finds a match:
        bypass pointer-map lookup for this source
    else under exclusive ProcGen protection:
        holder = lookup_by_full_source_pointer(source)
        if holder is absent:
            insert holder
            attempt generation into holder
            if visited reserve succeeds:
                store consumed source token
```

Source contract from [native availability map · functions](../experiments/native-availability-map/functions.csv) (line 16).

- **Inputs** — RCX ProcGen; RDX discrete Int2; R8 aggregate-output wrapper
- **Outputs** — Source-pointer cache attempted construction/reuse and related aggregate; no verified semantic return
- **Conditions** — Global current manager; ready context; bounded spatial source index; BIOM; components; definitions; lock and allocation
- **Effects** — Exclusive ProcGen+40; source-pointer map insertion; visited source+68; synchronous attempted production; raster and aggregate processing
- **Mapped edges** — 1407b5160/1407a1940 -> this ->14078c4e0/140985870/14079b520
- **Limits** — Source hash and visited key differ; provider return/publication not geometry completeness; not called by updater
- **Source assessment** — STATIC instruction/dataflow; STRONG scoped interpretation proposed

## RVA 0x007bc3d0

**Collect a runtime owner's supplemental resource lists** · Level A · preferred VA `0x1407bc3d0`

Traverses owner/type components and exchanges ordered supplemental arrays into a root-keyed map.

The caller supplies the explicit owner, retained store reader context and its own output map. The range uses owner in the high key bits and the live registered type word. Six nonzero payload words are appended in field order as allocation allows. A nonzero root exchanges the staging array with that root's destination, so repeated root entries can replace the destination and carry the previous array forward. Zero root keeps staging and final cleanup releases leftovers. The resulting component/root order is native container order. It establishes neither historical winning load order nor source-allocation lifetime. Full native declaration and general return are unknown.

## RVA 0x00982d90

**Find or allocate a Grid source** · Level A · preferred VA `0x140982d90`

Deduplicates or constructs a source object and attempts to publish it to the Grid.

In the qualified new-source branch, the allocation is `0x1a0` bytes. Grid `+0x5c` protection surrounds construction. SFBK `+0x00..+0x03`, geographic ancestry `+0x38..+0x63`, and token `+0x68..+0x6f` are initialized before vector publication. These fields and the source pointer are distinct from the ProcGen wrapper identity. A reused object is possible on the dedup path. Allocation failure precedes birth, and a nonnull result need not indicate vector publication after reserve failure. Full declaration, source reachability across all writers, and universal lifetime guarantees remain unknown.

## RVA 0x00988ab0

**Append a raw source pointer to the caller list** · Level B · preferred VA `0x140988ab0`

Copies one source-pointer value into the caller's temporary chunk list.

RCX carries caller list C. RDX points to the source-pointer value, of which the routine copies eight bytes. It allocates a 0x108-byte chunk through C's allocator or uses a spare chunk. EAX reports the prior unsigned 32-bit count. This mutates temporary list storage without acquiring a source reference.

## RVA 0x021e07d0

**Refresh Workshop area resource identities** · Level A · preferred VA `0x1421e07d0`

Analysis labels recorded in the original sources are `FUN_1421e07d0`.

Reads resident ProcGen geometry into Workshop area lists when refresh predicates require it.

The preview and established setup paths feed this updater with position/domain and radius/upper context. The local guard checks domain and translated displacement, with no separate radius or upper-scalar comparison. The qualified scan takes ProcGen shared protection at `+0x40`, traverses occupied source slots and calls `0x1421e0f50`. The scan has no provider call and does not imply a raster sample or a write to source ownership/visited state. Cache/list reuse can outlive or differ from geometry state. Existing executed consumer witnesses are scoped to their recorded setups. Complete callable prototype and return semantics remain unknown.

Scoped pseudocode abstraction. It covers the described branch and omits unqualified paths.

```text
Abstraction of the qualified consumer path. Translation arithmetic, conditions and cleanup are omitted.

when the established refresh guards require a scan:
    under ProcGen shared protection:
        for each occupied resident source slot:
            evaluate its regions against the Workshop query
            retain admitted resource identities
```

Source contract from [native availability map · functions](../experiments/native-availability-map/functions.csv) (line 2).

- **Inputs** — RCX state; RDX indexed XYZ/index/domain descriptor; XMM2 radius; XMM3 upper scalar
- **Outputs** — Three state vectors; AL refresh predicate
- **Conditions** — Initialized operation state; manager/world/ProcGen; translation table; source resolution; allocation
- **Effects** — Retains or replaces cached vectors; retains reference; locks ProcGen+40; allocates temporaries
- **Mapped edges** — 1421b2570/1421b41f0/1421b64c0 -> this -> 1421e0f50; ATMO and BIOM readers
- **Limits** — AL not set inequality; not unconditional fresh recompute; no returned complete set object
- **Source assessment** — STATIC instruction/dataflow; STRONG scoped interpretation proposed

This separate output-contract consumer reads populations for a particular selected harvester. Full inputs, return convention and general placement caller set remain unknown.

## RVA 0x027e5e80

**Remove task dependency edges after completion** · Level B · preferred VA `0x1427e5e80`

Applies dependency-category-specific removal and last-edge evaluation.

The category-zero path decrements node +0x90 at interior site 0x1427e61a4. The last dependency can evaluate a node in state 1 or 2 through virtual +0x08. Category one releases prerequisite references and may cancel an unneeded node that has yet to execute. Ready scan and worker dispatch are later boundaries.
